# Field sounds: event IDs, timing and hook ownership

Research date: 2026-10-16. Engine baseline: OpenSinnoh at `60b31ab`. Primary reference: [pret/pokeplatinum at `fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c`](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/tree/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c). Names, IDs, call sites and task behavior were consulted as reference facts; no decomp code, ROM data, audio or screenshots are included here.

The missing layer is mostly **native world event → sound**, rather than audio playback. `packages/audio` already decodes SDAT/SSEQ/SBNK/SWAR, sequences PCM/PSG/noise and sends output through the worklet. `packages/game/src/sound.ts` supplies deterministic `AudioHost`; `packages/vm/src/audio-ops.ts` already implements script `play_se`, `stop_se`, `wait_se`, fanfares and cries. `CreateWorld` in `packages/contracts/src/runtime.ts` does **not** receive audio, while the script runner and menu applications do. This explains why script-owned sounds can work while a door, jump or lift remains silent. Existing exceptions include the start-menu open cue (`game.ts`), explosion task audio (`vm/src/scene-ops.ts`), evolution and many Pokétch cues. Do not duplicate these while adding world hooks. See [existing audio research](audio.md), [gym tasks](gym-puzzles.md), [story scenes](story-scenes.md) and [Pokétch](poketch.md).

Systems: [movement](#movement-surfaces-and-avatar-actions), [doors/warps](#doors-stairs-escalators-and-warps), [HMs](#hms-obstacles-and-field-abilities), [weather](#ambient-weather-and-field-poison), [gyms/lifts](#gyms-moving-platforms-and-elevators), [transport](#boats-and-great-marsh-transport), [scenes](#explosions-and-camera-shake-scenes), [items](#script-audio-interaction-and-item-acquisition), [menus](#field-ui-menus-shop-and-pc), [other interactions/Pokétch](#other-field-interactions-and-application-sounds), [hook proposal](#world-and-vm-integration-proposal), [native index](#native-call-site-index), [script index](#appendix-all-explicit-field-script-se-and-fanfare-call-sites).

## Reading the mappings

- **IDs are SDAT sequence indexes** (`Sequence.sourceIndex`), not sample IDs, bank IDs or suffix numbers. The authoritative ID source is the ordered [`seqInfo` manifest](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/res/sound/pl_sound_data.json). Resolve the decomp **filename-based alias**, including duplicate suffixes, to that exact entry. Examples: `SE_CONFIRM_sseq_3` = **1500** (`SEQ_SE_DP_SELECT`); `SEQ_SE_PL_SUTYA2_sseq_3` = **1607** (`SEQ_SE_DP_SUTYA2`), whereas unsuffixed `SEQ_SE_PL_SUTYA2_sseq` = **1367**. Shared file bytes do not imply interchangeable bank/volume/player settings.
- Unless stated otherwise, a named field/main-task callback is one nominal **30 Hz tick** (1/30 second); use ticks for assertions. The DS clock-derived tick in contracts is about 33.43 ms. “Immediately” means in the same callback that performs the cited state change, with no authored delay. “N callbacks” starts at the named phase, not at the player's button press. Initialization, resource readiness, child-task scheduling, clip endpoints, fades and dialogue can add boundaries; no fixed total is claimed for those waits. The [main loop](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/main.c#L121-L166) runs application/main tasks before its VBlank synchronization and sound update.
- An SE starts without blocking unless an explicit poll is listed. `Sound_IsEffectPlaying(id)` asks whether **that sequence's player** has anything playing; it does not prove the originally started sequence is still active. Preserve that behavior for `wait_se` and Vs. Seeker. `stop_se` targets the sequence number, rather than stopping every field sound. A new sound on the same handle can replace the previous sound; do not introduce an arbitrary dedicated handle per world event. [Native effect operations](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/sound_playback.c), [script audio commands](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/scrcmd_sound.c).
- `PlayFanfare` is a separate ME-player operation: it pauses BGM and its completion poll includes **15 additional positive checks** after the player ends; the following check resumes BGM. Use imported `Sequence.frames` converted by `soundFramesToTicks`, not a guessed jingle duration or browser audio completion callback. The 15-check tail advances when polled, rather than independently while messages are open. The synth's roughly 192 Hz sound frames and 60 Hz sprite-animation delays are different clocks from the 30 Hz task counters. [Native fanfares](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/sound_playback.c).

The system tables below give event semantics and timing; the two call-site indexes make coverage auditable, including less common map scripts. Scope is the ordinary single-player overworld and its field menus, HMs, gyms, travel, weather, story tasks and Pokétch. Battle move SEs, multiplayer, Underground interior gameplay, contests and standalone minigames have separate controllers and are outside the native-event catalog; their field-script cues are still enumerated. An absent native ice/surface cue is recorded explicitly rather than guessed from a suggestive SDAT name. Clip-dependent timings and runtime sound operands stay identified as such.

## Movement, surfaces and avatar actions

| Event / condition | Sequence ID and decomp token | Call site / exact trigger |
|---|---|---|
| Snow step, including shadowed snow | **1353**, `SEQ_SE_PL_YUKI_sseq` | `PlayerAvatar_PlayWalkSE`, `player_move.c:301`: current underfoot tile passes snow classification. |
| Puddle step | **1601**, `SEQ_SE_DP_FOOT3_0_sseq` | Same function, :305. |
| Shallow water step | **1602**, `SEQ_SE_DP_FOOT3_1_sseq` | Same, :309. |
| Mud except deep mud | **1621**, `SEQ_SE_DP_MARSH_WALK_sseq` | Same, :315; deep-mud capture has its own task below. |
| Very tall grass | **1619**, `SEQ_SE_DP_KUSA_sseq` | Same, :322: either current or directional neighbor tile is very tall grass, except slow walking on the spot. |
| Ordinary ground / ordinary sand / normal short grass | **No generic footstep call here** | The sand classification in this function is unused. Do not emit a universal walking SE. Grass visual effects are a separate system. |
| Blocked attempted step | **1537**, `SEQ_SE_DP_WALL_HIT_sseq` | Walking/surfing `PlayerAvatar_SetMovement_NormalOverworld`, :1035/:1070; also cycling :1523/:1592/:1747/:1766/:1834, bike bridge :2561 and Distortion variants :1117/:1141/:1164/:1189. Emitted when starting the blocked movement action, not every held-input simulation tick. Normal overworld suppresses it when the collision contains `PLAYER_COLLISION_WARP`, which prevents a door bump before a warp. |
| Bike gear changes to gear 0 / gear 1 | **1564**, `SEQ_SE_DP_GEAR2_sseq` / **1561**, `SEQ_SE_DP_GEAR_sseq` | `PlayerAvatar_TryCyclingGearChange`, :1306/:1308; on the accepted B press, only cycling, excluded on bike ramp tiles. |
| Bike mount | **1560**, `SEQ_SE_DP_JITENSYA_sseq` | `PlayerAvatar_RequestStateCycle`, `overlay005/ov5_021DFB54.c:297`, when the transition applies. |
| Muddy bike slope forced-movement check | **1620**, `SEQ_SE_DP_SUNA_sseq` | `PlayerAvatar_TileMove_BikeSlope`, `player_move.c:611`, before direction/speed/collision branching. It is not a generic sand footstep. |
| Long gear-1 bike-ramp jump east / west | **1622**, `SEQ_SE_DP_DANSA4_sseq` | `PlayerAvatar_TileMove_BikeRampEast/West`, :671/:692, immediately before assigning the farther-jump action. Gear-0 near jumps lack this extra sound but retain generic jump sounds. |
| Ice sliding | **No ice-specific SE call in the native ice handler** | `PlayerAvatar_TileMove_Ice` and `PlayerAvatar_SetIceMovement`, `player_move.c:544–600,741–840`; snowball impacts below are audible. Do not conflate ice with the spin-slide loop. |
| Fuego Ironworks directional spin-slide | **1624**, `SEQ_SE_DP_F209_sseq`, explicit stop **1624, fade 0** | `ov5_021E11B0`, `overlay005/ov5_021E1154.c:65`: start once when the task is created. The task pauses animation, starts a normal 8-update step, and performs a 7-callback spin (turns when the remaining counter is 6,4,2). On the final countdown, choose the tile arrow or next rotational direction; loop if clear, otherwise stop sound in `ov5_021E120C:144` immediately before finishing. Do not restart the loop every tile. |

Walking/material timing: `PlayerAvatar_MoveMain` first rejects a move that cannot begin, initializes and chooses the accepted movement action, then calls `PlayerAvatar_PlayWalkSE` once (`player_move.c:148–181`). The helper only sounds while `AVATAR_MOVE_STATE_MOVING`. Forced tile movements return before that helper. Preserve this ordering: sound when the next movement action is accepted, using the native current/directional-neighbor classification, not on landing and not from renderer animation frames. The independent surface predicates can produce more than one call.

Sources: [movement dispatch and surfaces](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/player_move.c#L148-L325), [slope/ramp/ice](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/player_move.c#L544-L840), [normal movement/collision](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/player_move.c#L1014-L1196), [gear](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/player_move.c#L1284-L1311), [spin-slide task](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay005/ov5_021E1154.c#L33-L151).

### Generic jumps: ledges, script jumps and HM hops share the mechanism

`MovementAction_InitJumpCustomSound` emits its nonzero sound at **movement initialization**; the ordinary wrapper supplies **1547 `SEQ_SE_DP_DANSA_sseq`** (`unk_020655F4.c:787–816`). `MovementAction_Jump_Step1` emits landing **1607 `SEQ_SE_PL_SUTYA2_sseq_3`** on the update that decrements remaining duration to zero (:870). This also applies to NPC/script movement actions using these primitives. A second Distortion jump updater emits that same landing ID at :1935. Hook the movement lifecycle once, rather than separately adding takeoff sounds to the player, VM ledge instruction and each HM.

| Action family | Motion duration at 30 Hz | Takeoff / landing |
|---|---:|---|
| Jump on spot slow; near slow; far (ordinary ledge: two tiles) | **16 updates** | 1547 at init; 1607 at completion. |
| Jump on spot fast; near fast (Surf and Rock Climb hops) | **8 updates** | Same pair. |
| Farther bike jump | **12 updates** | Same pair, plus 1622 from the gear-1 ramp handler. |
| Distortion far jump created by the custom-sound primitive | **24 updates** | Explicit `SEQ_NONE` at init, so no 1547 from that call; generic completion still emits 1607. |

The initializer returns a continuation result, so the movement executor may begin its update in that same engine pass; specify regression assertions relative to movement-init and the Nth update, rather than assuming an extra setup tick. [Jump init/update/action tables](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/unk_020655F4.c#L787-L1024), [Distortion landing](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/unk_020655F4.c#L1924-L1937).

## Doors, stairs, escalators and warps

| Event | Sequence | Call site / timing |
|---|---|---|
| Hinged door opening | **1541 `SEQ_SE_DP_DOOR_OPEN_sseq`** | `DoorAnimation_PlayOpenAnimation`, `overlay005/ov5_021D431C.c:759–787`: same call that starts the prop clip. Automatic entrance :146/:158 and exit :314/:326 select the same ID. |
| Sliding door opening | **1544 `SEQ_SE_DP_DOOR10_sseq`** | Same selector; sliding model classes: Pokémon Center outer and inner, Gym, GTS inside, card door, elevator door (:466–476). |
| Veilstone Department Store door | **1492 `SEQ_SE_PL_DOOR_OPEN5_sseq`** | Store model gets its distinct opening chime. |
| Hinged door closing | **1543 `SEQ_SE_DP_DOOR_CLOSE2_sseq`** | `DoorAnimation_PlayCloseAnimation:790–817`; automatic entrance :194/:206 and exit :365/:377. Start with closing clip, after the automatic walk-through has completed. |
| Sliding / store door closing | **None (ID 0 passed)** | Intentional: do not add the hinged closing SE. |
| Pokémon Center escalators | **1557 `SEQ_SE_DP_ESUKA`** | `ov5_021D4858` starts sound with prop animation in state 1 (:528); `ov5_021D4A24` does likewise :643. Explicit stop **1557,0** when both animation and screen fade finish (:587/:689). It is a task lifetime sound, not a one-shot per step. |
| General script map-change fade | **1539 `SEQ_SE_DP_KAIDAN2_sseq`** | `FieldTask_ChangeMap`, `field_map_change.c:631`: state 0, before fading out/finishing old field. Colosseum path :1516 has the same call. |
| Stair/ordinary transition fallback | **1539** | `sub_02056DE4`, `unk_02056B30.c:263`: with fade-out initiation; `sub_02056F1C` :366 after its entry walking animation completes, before fade-out. `ov5_021D4F14`/following helper, `ov5_021D431C.c:949/:980` use it with their fade-out. These are native transition paths; do not also sound every logical map load. |
| Escape Rope / Dig | **1539** | `StartWarpOutSpinning`, `overlay006/field_warp.c:172/:175`, when initial spin animation starts. Dig first waits for HM cut-in; Escape Rope does not. |
| Teleport | **1614 `SEQ_SE_DP_TELE_sseq`** | Same task, :178, when spin starts after the HM cut-in. Warp-in state table has no additional SE call. |
| Vertical spin warp presentation | **1615 `SEQ_SE_DP_TELE2_sseq`** | `FieldSystem_StartWarpAnimation` callbacks in `overlay005/ov5_021E135C.c:57/:98`, first callback of departure and arrival. Departure counter starts 0; increment-before-test starts its6-tick black fade on callback 20 (offset19 from the first sound); arrival starts fade on its second callback, then continues through counter 20 and waits for fade completion. Distinct from Dig/Teleport's `field_warp.c` task. |
| Fly | **1609 `SEQ_SE_DP_FW019_sseq`** plus selected Pokémon cry in the shared cut-in | `HMCutIn_PlaySwooshSound`, `overlay006/hm_cut_in.c:1464`: waits for the bird task's substate **3** (bird is ready for descent), then plays the swoosh on that transition. Do not play at menu selection or at map load. |

The prop manager emits a supplied sound only when its ID is nonzero (`map_prop_animation.c:763`), in the same operation that starts the one-shot clip. Door waits use clip completion, not sound completion. Automatic entry waits for open clip, walks north, then hides the player and starts close; automatic exit starts the fade with the opening sound, waits for open clip before showing/walking the player, then closes. Clip durations belong to imported animation metadata, not an invented fixed sound delay. [Door selection and lifecycle](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay005/ov5_021D431C.c#L71-L403), [model classes, escalators and script door API](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay005/ov5_021D431C.c#L466-L817), [general map change](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/field_map_change.c#L623-L655), [Rope/Dig/Teleport](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay006/field_warp.c#L160-L241), [vertical warp](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay005/ov5_021E135C.c#L48-L129), [Fly swoosh](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay006/hm_cut_in.c#L1420-L1472).

## HMs, obstacles and field abilities

All ordinary HM cut-ins play the selected Pokémon's **cry**, not a generic HM effect, at `HMCutIn_SlideMonToCenter`, `hm_cut_in.c:1238`. On reaching the center, the decelerating horizontal delta is tested after division by two, and the cry fires when it becomes greater than −2 units (the sixth callback of this phase). The cut-in does not wait for cry completion. With the sprite-transfer readiness satisfied on the callback after init, this is callback **36** of the cut-in; total presentation is **59** callbacks in the current world's same readiness assumption. Delaying resource readiness moves the cry and all later phases together. Do not attach the cry to `play_hm_cut_in` dispatch or obstacle creation. [Cut-in dispatch/phases](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay006/hm_cut_in.c#L1085-L1329).

| Event | Sequence | Call site / timing |
|---|---|---|
| Cut slash | **1611 `SEQ_SE_DP_FW015_sseq`** | Obstacle effect constructor `ov6_0224825C`, `overlay006/ov6_02248050.c:211`; called by destroy kind 0 after HM cut-in. Same tick the original effect is instantiated. Field/menu scripts then wait **7 ticks** before removing tree object, and poll completion separately. No SE wait. |
| Rock Smash | **1610 `SEQ_SE_DP_FW088_sseq`** | Constructors `ov6_02248340:290` (kind 1, player-facing) and `ov6_022483A0:309` (kind 2, object-facing). Field/menu scripts wait **10 ticks** before deleting the obstacle; effect completion is separate. Do not delay sound to object deletion. |
| Strength activation | Selected-mon cut-in cry, no separate activation SE in script | `scripts_field_moves.s:FieldMoves_UseStrengthFromField/FromMenu`; activation flag and messages surround the cut-in. |
| Each successful Strength push | **1575 `SEQ_SE_DP_UG_023_sseq`** | Push task `ov5_021DFFBC`, `ov5_021DFB54.c:579`: after both actor animations are ready, assigns boulder slow walk (**16 updates**) and player normal walk-on-spot (**8 updates**), then sounds once; waits for both. Failed pushes do not reach this call. |
| Rock Climb mount spawn | **1575** | `SubTask_RockClimb_CreateMount`, `ov5_021DFB54.c:1086`, after cut-in and before hop-on. Both hop-on and hop-off use the generic 8-update jump sounds. |
| Rock Climb debris cadence | **1575** repeatedly | `ov6_02248610`, `ov6_02248050.c:503`: each emitted debris effect. `ov6_022488D8` decrements a zero-initialized counter, emits on first update, resets to 4, then emits every **5 callbacks** while the effect manager runs. Effect manager is destroyed when the task schedules hop-off. Distinct from its single mount-spawn sound; not one emission per map tile. |
| Waterfall after cut-in | **1613 `SEQ_SE_DP_FW463_sseq`** | `SubTask_Waterfall_WaitForAscentCutIn` / `WaitForDescentCutIn`, `ov5_021DFB54.c:1236/:1353`: immediately when cut-in completion is observed. This is the first of two1613 starts on the cut-in path; automatic no-cut-in descent skips this first call. Native traversal itself has **32 + 64 updates**. |
| Separate Waterfall splash effect | **1613** | `ov6_02248498`, `ov6_02248050.c:408`: synchronous effect constructor. `SubTask_Waterfall_InitAscent` (`ov5_021DFB54.c:1270`) and `InitDescent` (`:1388`) call `ov6_022485F4`, whose constructor emits1613. Therefore the cut-in path plays 1613 **again on the next field-task callback after Wait*CutIn**, while automatic descent still plays this constructor cue once. Preserve both starts where the native path makes both calls; do not deduplicate them. |
| Surf mount/dismount | Cut-in cry for entering + generic jump pair | Traversal task in `ov5_021DFB54.c`; no extra dedicated Surf SE call exists there. Mount and dismount near-fast jumps use 1547/1607. Surf BGM is separate. |
| Defog | **1612 `SEQ_SE_DP_FBRADE_sseq`** | `FieldMoves_UseDefogFromMenu`, `scripts_field_moves.s:386`, after HM cut-in and setting Defog active; before weather transition command. The unused interaction path has no such call. |
| Flash | Shared cut-in cry only in field-move script | `FieldMoves_UseFlashFromMenu`: sets Flash active, starts light change, waits **42 ticks**; no extra explicit SE. |
| Deep-mud capture / escape | **1617 `SEQ_SE_DP_ZUPO_sseq`** / **1618 `SEQ_SE_DP_ZUPO2_sseq`** | `FieldTask_StuckInDeepMud`, `ov5_021DFB54.c:897/:907`: first task state plays capture and falls through; escape SE after five accepted direction changes **and** the final 8-update on-spot hop has finished. Generic hop sounds also apply. No periodic sound for every stuck input. |
| Snowpoint snowball impact | **1627 `SEQ_SE_DP_FW291_sseq`** | `ov5_021E06A8`, `ov5_021DFB54.c:996`: forced movement, animation set, speed ≥1, facing object gfx 0x76. Spawn break effect, delete live object, then play immediately; nonblocking, occupancy clears and slide continues. |
| Sweet Scent | **1608 `SEQ_SE_DP_FW230_sseq`** | `ov5_021F007C:70` / `ov5_021F013C:123`, `overlay005/ov5_021F007C.c`: beginning of the tint/alpha effect after shared HM cut-in (`ov5_021F101C`). Reverse fade constructors do not sound. Native weather eligibility decides whether the effect path runs. |
| Fishing cast | **1616 `SEQ_SE_DP_FW104_sseq`** | `FishingTask_CastRod`, `overlay005/fishing.c:235`: increment cast counter, emit when it equals **10**. Cast lasts **34 callbacks**, then enters wait in that same tick. The bite creates a sound-enabled shared emote (1501), described below; there is no separate fishing-only bite ID. Hook the tenth cast update, not rod selection or bite. |
| Vs. Seeker | **1568 `SE_DP_VS_SEEKER_BEEP_sseq`** (SYMB `SEQ_SE_DP_UG_005`) | `VsSeeker_ExecuteTask`, `overlay005/vs_seeker.c:430`: starts player animation and beep only after usability succeeds. Waits for player and rematch animations, then also waits until **that SE player is no longer playing** (:452), before removing the player task. Failure branches do not beep. |

Sources: [Cut/Smash/Strength scripts](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/res/field/scripts/scripts_field_moves.s#L47-L244), [Defog/Flash](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/res/field/scripts/scripts_field_moves.s#L377-L407), [destroy-kind dispatch and completion flag](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay006/ov6_02248948.c#L24-L75), [obstacle/traversal effects](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay006/ov6_02248050.c#L196-L409), [Rock Climb debris](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay006/ov6_02248050.c#L489-L674), [Strength/mud/snowball](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay005/ov5_021DFB54.c#L551-L998), [Rock Climb/Waterfall](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay005/ov5_021DFB54.c#L1054-L1390), [fishing](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay005/fishing.c#L208-L249), [Vs. Seeker](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay005/vs_seeker.c#L411-L456).

### Hook proposal for the current world/VM

- `Avatar.input` and accepted native step setup own material sounds, bump, gear and ramp calls; `Avatar.startSlide` and task completion own 1624 start/stop; `Avatar`'s Strength/mud/Rock Climb/Waterfall phase transitions own their sounds. The current `AvatarHost` lacks an audio dependency. Inject contracts' `AudioHost` at world construction, resolve source sequence indexes through the world's registries, and pass the small play/stop/poll helper to actor hosts. Do not import audio engine internals into world.
- `npc.ts` / common actor motion creation and completion own generic takeoff/landing, including VM `apply_movement`, NPC movement and avatar hop/ledge paths. A direct player-only hook would miss script jumps; duplicated avatar/VM hooks would double-play them. Carry a per-motion optional takeoff override/disabled marker for the custom silent Distortion jump. Movement source `script` is not a reason to suppress native jump sounds.
- `World.playDoor` should choose a **recorded door sound class/model mapping** and emit with the actual open/close clip start. Importer stores class metadata; this is importer output, so implementation must bump `IMPORTER_VERSION`. Map-change transitions need explicit sound ownership to avoid layering a default stair SE over a door SE; escalator loop stops when its own animation and fade settle.
- `FieldHost.destroyObstacle` owns effect-constructor SEs. VM already executes explicit script `play_se` and `wait_time`; preserve those positions. A request to start the obstacle is the right slash/smash sound point, not `RemoveObject` seven or ten ticks later. Common HM cut-in presentation needs a timed cry hook (callback 36 under current 59-tick assumption); world traversal's synthetic cut-in timer and VM `play_hm_cut_in` must share that presenter.
- `FishingTask.step` should emit 1616 on its tenth `cast` update. Its current countdown starts at 34, so the trigger is the cast update whose decrement reaches **24** (the tenth), while retaining the current same-tick cast→wait chaining.
- Terrain and weather presentation are deterministic game events: renderer animation frame sampling cannot be their source. Owned looping effects need explicit stop on task/map teardown as well as normal completion.

## Ambient weather and field poison

| Event | Sequence / owner | Timing / lifecycle |
|---|---|---|
| Rain ambience | **1593 `SEQ_SE_DP_T_AME`** | `ov5_021D7658`, `overlay005/ov5_021D5EB8.c:2106/:2133`: start once for weather entering or direct initialization. Stop :2152 when leaving state starts. |
| Heavy rain ambience | **1595 `SEQ_SE_DP_T_OOAME`** | `ov5_021D9984:3878` / `ov5_021D9A58:3915`; stop in `ov5_021D9B28:3937` at transition out. The weather's common free/replacement paths at :1233/:1272 also stop the owned ambient SE via `ov5_021DB4E4` (:5289). |
| Thunder | **1597 `SEQ_SE_DP_T_KAMI2_sseq`** (2/3), **1596 `SEQ_SE_DP_T_KAMI_sseq`** (1/3) | `ov5_021DA748:4599/:4603`, when a lightning cycle begins. Callback initializes brightness 0 and slope `200 + rand%480`; short/slow decay uses decrement36 and delay `floor(slope/2)+rand%120`; fast decay uses decrement200 and delay `rand%15`. There are two brightness-up callbacks before decay. Delay processing uses a `>=0` countdown, so the next cycle starts after its selected delay plus one countdown callback. Audio is on flash-cycle start, not a fixed interval or delayed until brightness peaks. |
| Field poison flash | **1552 `SEQ_SE_DP_DOKU2_sseq`** | `Field_DoPoisonEffect`, `overlay005/ov5_021EF4BC.c:76`, exactly when flash task is started. Native effect has no audio wait. Hook the field poison visual/event initiation once, not each poisoned party member. |

[Rain lifecycle](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay005/ov5_021D5EB8.c#L2089-L2154), [heavy rain lifecycle](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay005/ov5_021D5EB8.c#L3869-L3938), [thunder state machine](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay005/ov5_021D5EB8.c#L4563-L4638), [poison initiation](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay005/ov5_021EF4BC.c#L60-L77).

Weather proposal: `World.setWeather`/map-weather application own ambient start/stop and a seeded lightning task owns flash+thunder together; any existing renderer thunder RNG must be replaced or supplied deterministic events. `weather.ts` currently resolves weather state but has no audio lifetime owner. Avoid adding an invented light-rain1594 loop merely because SDAT contains it: this source uses only 1593/1595 through the shared ambience owner.

## Gyms, moving platforms and elevators

The gym-specific native calls are in [`overlay008/gym_features.c`](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay008/gym_features.c). Unless stated otherwise these tasks advance once per 30 Hz world tick, stop a loop with zero fade, and wait for movement/animation rather than for the ending SE. The numeric IDs below are SDAT SEQ slots, not the position among nonempty SEQs.

| System/event | SE symbol and numeric ID | Exact native call site and timing |
|---|---|---|
| Oreburgh | No puzzle-specific SE | `scripts_oreburgh_city_gym.s` has ordinary interaction confirmation calls at 11/80/99; no native moving-puzzle feature. Do not invent a rock, button or lift cue for this gym. |
| Eterna clock rotation | `SEQ_SE_PL_TOKEI21_sseq` **1355**, then `SEQ_SE_DP_PIRORIRO2_sseq` **1507** | `FieldTask_EternaGym_AdvanceClockState`, gym_features.c **2769**, **2797–2798**: after the camera reaches the clock, state 2 increments a zero-based delay until **8**; that eighth callback starts 1355 and **falls through to the first rotation update in the same callback**. Stop 1355 and play 1507 in the callback that reaches the target time. The trainer rotations take **325/90/105 rotation updates**, the leader **23**, so with start callback `t=0` the respective stop/chime offsets are **324/89/104/22 ticks**, not the full update counts. |
| Eterna fountain drains | `SEQ_SE_DP_T_AME_sseq` **1593** | Same task **2860/2871**: after camera movement, state 9 waits **4** increment-before-test callbacks, switches the chosen fountain to draining and starts 1593. State 10 waits **60** callbacks then stops 1593, starts the message and waits for print/input. Stop is exactly **60 ticks after start**; do not stop on animation completion or keep it playing while the prompt waits. The clock chime has no SE wait. |
| Hearthome Platinum | No separate puzzle/light/incorrect-door SE in this feature | Native feature handles clue placement, lights, door selection and trainer vision without a direct sound call. Doors use the shared door/warp mechanism; trainer approach owns its emote/BGM path described below. Distinguish the unused DP lift described below. |
| Veilstone unsuccessful bag push | `SEQ_SE_PL_SUTYA2_sseq` **1367** | `FieldTask_VeilstoneGym_HitPunchingBagNoMovement`, **3267**: once the player animation accepts a movement, begin walk-on-spot and the nonmoving bag animation and play SE in that callback. Wait for both animations, with no sound wait. |
| Veilstone kick/move | `SEQ_SE_PL_SUTYA2_sseq` **1367**, `SEQ_SE_PL_GYM01_sseq` **1356** | `VeilstoneGym_AnimationState_KickBag`, **3334**, plays 1367 and returns CONTINUE, so the first acceleration update happens **in the same callback**. `StartMovement`, **3355**, starts 1356 when elapsedFrames increments to **30**: with accepted kick at `t=0`, this is **t=29**. |
| Veilstone bag stop/impact | Stop **1356**, play **1367** and `SEQ_SE_PL_GYM02_sseq` **1357** | `VeilstoneGym_AnimationState_MovePunchingBag`, **3397–3399**, on the first crossed tile boundary having nonzero tile flags. Sound follows the collision/pause-point detection and exact bag snap, not a fixed duration. All three audio commands occur in this order in one callback, even when no tire stack is hit. |
| Veilstone tires topple | `SEQ_SE_DP_UG_026_sseq` **1578** | `VeilstoneGym_AnimationState_ToppleTireStack`, **3428**: impact sets elapsedFrames=0 and returns ADVANCE_FRAME; the **fourth following callback** starts the topple clip and 1578 (`impact+4 ticks`). Tire removal waits for the clip only. |
| Pastoria buttons/water | `SEQ_SE_DP_FW056_sseq` **1626** | `PastoriaGym_UpdateButtonAnimations`, **768**, starts SE with pressed/unpressed button clips, **before water starts moving**. Orange/blue states wait until all three button clips finish, then move water **1 world unit per callback**; low/middle/high are **0/32/64 units**. Stop is in the **next callback after the endpoint update**, with plate/collision committed: orange **520**, blue **637**. Green chooses direction in a separate state-1 callback, moves next callback and stops in state 4 **594**, one callback after reaching 32. The map frame-script conditions prevent retriggering the already-selected colour (the button scripts maintain map-local vars1–3); the native press function itself does not reject it. Green's already-at-middle branch frees the task without reaching its stop case, so do not describe this unreachable ordinary-script path as a normal silent no-op. |
| Canalave vertical platform | `SEQ_SE_DP_ELEBETA_sseq` **1553**, then `SEQ_SE_DP_KI_GASYAN_sseq` **1562** | `FieldTask_CanalaveGym_MovePlatformUpDown`, **1346/1362/1370/1384**: start motor in state 0; move **8 world units/tick** from the next callback; stop motor on exact endpoint callback; play 1562 in next callback (state 2), then finish one callback later. Thus a vertical distance D has motor stop offset `ceil(D/8)` and clunk offset `ceil(D/8)+1`, relative to motor start. No SE wait. |
| Canalave east/west platform | **1553**, then **1562** | `FieldTask_CanalaveGym_MovePlatformEastWest`, **1407/1424/1435/1456**: start motor only when the player's animation can accept PAUSE_ANIMATION; each step follows the player's WALK_FASTER_EAST/WEST movement. Stop when the observed player/platform x reaches its target. Resume animation in state 2; its deliberate fallthrough permits state 3 to play the clunk in that same callback **if** the resume animation already reports finished. Otherwise the clunk waits. |
| Canalave north/south platform | **1553**, then **1562** | `FieldTask_CanalaveGym_MovePlatformNorthSouth`, **1480/1497/1508/1532**, analogous destination/animation gating, but state 2 **breaks**, so the clunk cannot be checked until a following callback. Preserve this east/west versus north/south distinction instead of a generic fixed end delay. |
| Snowpoint snowball breaks | `SEQ_SE_DP_FW291_sseq` **1627** | `ov5_021E06A8` in `overlay005/ov5_021DFB54.c` **996**: only if player speed>=1, facing object exists and gfx=0x76; start the break effect, delete the snowball, then play SE in that callback. Rejected slow/invalid collisions have no break cue. Ice motion/landing itself belongs to shared player movement. |
| Sunyshore gear buttons | `SEQ_SE_DP_GAGAGA_sseq` **1599** | `SunyshoreGym_PressButton`, **2184**, starts once after updating rotation state and before installing gear task. `FieldTask_SunyshoreGym_RotateGears` advances **5.625 degrees/update**: **16 updates** for normal/reverse 90 degrees, **32** for double 180 degrees. There is **no explicit stop, arrival cue or sound wait**; let the SE complete. Do not replay it once per gear or on re-entry/reconstruction. |
| Hearthome DP leftover lift (not Platinum route) | **1553**, then **1562** | `HearthomeGymDP_RaiseLift` **804/817/827**; LowerLift **846/858/868**: motor at state 0, position ±1 unit/callback starting next tick, motor stop at clamp, clunk one tick after clamp. Its presence in the shared source does not justify enabling a Platinum puzzle that uses this lift. Legacy DP scripts separately use `SEQ_SE_DP_UG_020_sseq` **1572** and `SEQ_SE_DP_MAZYO4_sseq` **1591**; keep them tied to those map scripts. |

Sources: [Pastoria](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay008/gym_features.c#L488-L769), [Canalave](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay008/gym_features.c#L1334-L1545), [Sunyshore](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay008/gym_features.c#L2141-L2250), [Eterna](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay008/gym_features.c#L2745-L2898), [Veilstone](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay008/gym_features.c#L3250-L3440), [Snowpoint break](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay005/ov5_021DFB54.c#L968-L999).

**Other platform lifts.** [`src/platform_lift.c:351–500`](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/platform_lift.c#L351-L500): Iron Island uses **1553 ELEBETA**, League uses `SEQ_SE_DP_ELEBETA4_sseq` **1556**. Start in START_MOVING, stop in the callback clamping the target y, clunk **1562** in FINISH_MOVING one tick later, then complete one tick after that. Iron Island rises **1 unit/tick**, League rises **2**, every downward ride uses **1**. With motor start `t=0`, endpoint/stop is `t=ceil(abs(deltaY)/speed)` and clunk `t+1`. `preventGoingDown` at top returns before allocating a task or playing any sound. The saved destination floor flips at trigger time, not sound time.

**Interior elevators.** [`src/overlay006/elevator_animation.c:55–117`](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay006/elevator_animation.c#L55-L115): first callback loads the prop animation; next plays the selected up/down light animation **and** `SEQ_SE_DP_ELEBETA2_sseq` **1554** (same SE both directions). On the callback observing the requested clip loops finished, stop 1554 and play `SEQ_SE_DP_PINPON_sseq` **1521** in the same callback. **Wait until PINPON is no longer playing**, then one cleanup callback. Clip duration/loop count, rather than floors times an invented constant, controls motor duration.

**Hook proposal.** Add a narrow audio capability to `FeatureHost` in `packages/world/src/gyms/feature.ts` and pass the game's existing `AudioHost` through `FieldWorld.makeFeatureHost()`. Put each start/stop directly in `gyms/{eterna,veilstone,pastoria,canalave}.ts` (and the future Sunyshore controller)'s matching simulation state transition; `world.ts.breakSnowball()` owns the snowball call. `PlatformLift` should receive that capability or emit commands consumed in the same `stepPlatformLift()` tick. The existing `world.ts.elevator()`/completion task should own 1554/1521 and its SE wait. Initialization, `view()`, renderer interpolation and reconstruction from a save must never synthesize these one-shot events. Resolve `sourceIndex` to a pack sequence once through registries; do not assume a caller can use a decomp symbol as an existing record id.

## Boats and Great Marsh transport

| Event | Sound and ID | Timing/call site |
|---|---|---|
| Canalave departure | `SEQ_SE_DP_SHIP02_sseq` **1757** | `FieldSystem_PlayBoatCutscene`, `boat_cutscene.c:128`: only NORTH_TO_SOUTH, when bridge animation resources are loaded and the pre-fade departure task is established. It starts at setup, before the first movement callback. Snowpoint's pre-fade steamship departure does not use this cue. |
| Canalave bridge opening | `SEQ_SE_DP_SHIP03_sseq` **1758** | `FieldSystem_PlayBoatCutsceneStep`, **205**, on the callback that starts both bridge animations after reaching the **14-tile bookkeeping distance**, once per trip. Boat position moves with the current speed; speed increases from .25 to 1.25 **after the 24th position update**, and that callback adds the new speed to the distance counter. Consequently the bridge threshold is met on departure movement update **198** (bookkeeping distance 224.5 units; actual displacement 223.5). It is not at actual ship z=224. The 25-tile goal is met on update **339**; fade still waits for both bridge clips. |
| Both standalone ship travel cinematics | `SEQ_SE_DP_SHIP01_sseq` **1756** | `BoatCutscene_CanalaveShip_Main` and `BoatCutscene_SnowpointShip_Main`, `canalave_ship.c:143`, `snowpoint_ship.c:143`: first main callback, before advancing the cinematic model animations; not delayed until the fade-in completes. First animation reaching its final frame starts the exit fade. No explicit SE stop/wait. |
| Great Marsh ride preparation | `SEQ_SE_DP_TRAIN04_sseq` **1755** | `MoveTram`, `great_marsh_tram.c:212`: state 1 only for RIDE, then **falls through** to `Sound_IsEffectPlaying(1755)` in state 2. Movement starts only on a later state 3 callback after that player is clear. CALL does not play it but still performs the is-playing check. |
| Great Marsh riding acceleration | `SEQ_SE_DP_TRAIN02_sseq` **1753** | `RideTramToArea`, **294/313**: when accepting the movement for speed level0 while still before the slowdown z, immediately before incrementing speed level. Driven by actual player-animation completion, not a periodic timer. |
| Great Marsh riding brakes | `SEQ_SE_DP_TRAIN03_sseq` **1754** | `RideTramToArea`, **301/320**: at/after slowdown z, when current speed level is its maximum and the next decrement is selected. |
| Great Marsh summoned tram brakes | **1754** | `CallTramToPlayer`, **253/266**: after reaching slowdown z, when the elapsed frames at speed level0 reach **2 callbacks** and level increments toward slower movement; no separate call/start cue. |

Sources: [boat setup/state](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/cutscenes/boat_cutscene/boat_cutscene.c#L78-L245), [speed counter](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/cutscenes/boat_cutscene/boat_cutscene.c#L260-L335), [Canalave cinematic](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/cutscenes/boat_cutscene/canalave_ship.c#L120-L168), [Snowpoint cinematic](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/cutscenes/boat_cutscene/snowpoint_ship.c#L120-L168), [tram](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay006/great_marsh_tram.c#L191-L329).

**Hook proposal.** Extend `BoatHost` in `packages/world/src/boat.ts` with the same audio capability; play 1757 at accepted departure creation, 1758 at `bridgeReached`'s first transition, 1756 on entry to the cinematic's first main callback. Preserve the cinematic-start event separately from its total duration even while rendering is stubbed. `BoatTrip` already models the .25/1.25 bookkeeping asymmetry. Snowpoint movement/cinematic are currently explicitly stubbed and should not receive guessed Canalave cues. The Great Marsh tram task should own whistle completion, acceleration and braking events; its existing feature/VM movement wait must include the whistle SE wait.

## Explosions and camera-shake scenes

`ScrCmd_29F` (`scrcmd.c:6374–6386`) branches on its resolved var: zero calls `ov6_0223E384`, nonzero calls `ov6_0223E4EC`; both nest a blocking field task. Shared camera-shake setup `ov6_0223FE1C` plays `SEQ_SE_DP_CLIMAX09_sseq` **1749** (`overlay006/ov6_0223E140.c:1853`) **at command/setup time**. There is no stop/wait on 1749. Mode0 shakes amplitude 2 for 16 four-update cycles, restoring the camera afterwards; it does not play the explosion loop. Mode1: fade BGM out over **1 tick**, poll completion, set delay 16; after **16 decrement-before-test callbacks**, play `SEQ_SE_DP_FW089_sseq` **1628** (**569**). Then shake amplitude 4 for 24 cycles, reducing amplitude by an eighth at cycle boundaries; on shake completion, stop **1628** with fade0 (**577**). Wait another 16 callbacks, start BGM fade-in 16, wait for fade completion, then cleanup. These are phase-relative counts: the BGM poll and switch dispatch add callbacks; do not flatten to a guessed global timestamp. Mode1's actual loop stop tracks the camera helper's completed state, not the SSEQ duration. [Command branch](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/scrcmd.c#L6374-L6386), [mode 1 task/setup](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay006/ov6_0223E140.c#L548-L624), [shake helper](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay006/ov6_0223E140.c#L1820-L1985).

The Canalave library scene first waits for ordinary confirmation SE, displays an instant message, then invokes mode 1 (`scripts_canalave_library_3f.s:134–136`). Do not play the explosion at the start of its long conversation or independently from the opcode. **This sound path already exists in our VM**: `packages/vm/src/scene-ops.ts` defines 1749/1628 and starts/stops them through `r.audio`, including fades and delays. The task here is to preserve/audit that implementation, not add duplicate world playback. Other explicit scripted explosion cues belong to the existing `play_se`/`stop_se` commands and their actual script position. [Library call](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/res/field/scripts/scripts_canalave_library_3f.s#L118-L139).

## Distortion World

The separate portal application `DWWarp_Main` plays **1491 `SEQ_SE_PL_SYUWA2_sseq`** at `dw_warp/dw_warp.c:120`: after the initial screen wipe, its LOOP phase tests the zero-initialized counter against15 **before** incrementing, so sound starts on LOOP callback 16 (offset15). The loop exits only when its incremented frame counter exceeds85, on callback 86; the next CLEAR_SCREEN callback starts a20-tick fade, then WAIT polls it. No explicit SE stop/wait. Hook the application phase, not the initiating warp request. [Portal timeline](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/dw_warp/dw_warp.c#L105-L142).

All native sites below are in [`overlay009/ov9_02249960.c`](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay009/ov9_02249960.c). DW tasks have their own event-command state machine; field scripts starting them must wait on that task instead of trying to reproduce timing with adjacent `play_se` commands. The sound helper at **9749–9754**, `PlaySoundIfNotActive` (older name `ov9_02265DB0`), calls play only if `Sound_IsEffectPlaying(seqID)` is false. Preserve that gate: many simultaneous disappearing props share one sound; it is not one voice/retrigger per prop.

| Event | SE symbol / numeric ID | Exact trigger / stop |
|---|---|---|
| Land on a floating floor/wall/ceiling support | `SEQ_SE_PL_SUTYA2_sseq_3` **1607** (SDAT named `SEQ_SE_DP_SUTYA2`) | Floating-platform jump task **2893**, only after `TickJumpOnFloatingPlatformMovementAnimation` returns complete, new support/orientation applied and dust effect requested; before FINISH's facing-animation acceptance. This is the **landing**, not the start of a jump. Do not confuse plain SUTYA2 **1367** (Veilstone) with the `_3` alias. |
| Ghost platform appear/disappear | `SEQ_SE_PL_SYUWA3_sseq` **1484** | `DistWorldPlatformProp_AnimTick` **3740/3748**, on the prop's SFX-state transition to disappearing/appearing while opacity actually changes. Goes through helper. Opacity update and sound are simulation behavior even if the prop is culled from rendering. |
| Ghost rock appear or disappear | `SEQ_SE_PL_FW089_2_sseq` **1483** | `DistWorldObstacleProp_AnimInit` assigns both sound ids **3792–3793** for LAND_ROCK. `AnimTick` **3861/3877** requests through helper only when the SFX-state changes. Disappear first reverses animation2 frames/update and waits until opacity>=current animation frame before decrementing opacity/starting sound; appearance starts on first opacity increase. |
| Other ghost obstacles appear/disappear | `SEQ_SE_PL_MEKI_sseq` **1485** / `SEQ_SE_PL_MEKI2_sseq` **1486** | Init assignments **3795–3796**, same dynamic calls **3861/3877**. No explicit stop for these obstacle sequences. |
| Giratina-room elevator appears | **1484** | `DistWorldMovingPlatformProp_AnimTick` **6262**, when stepping-stones flag is set and opacity is still minimum; helper gates retries. It increments animStep and takes opacity=`animStep>>1`; because this leaves opacity0 on the first update, do not turn this into an unconditional repeated play. |
| Floor-to-floor elevator/platform trip | `SEQ_SE_PL_FW089_sseq` **1481** | `DistWorldElevatorPlatform_BeginMovement` **5275**, on first vibration only (`vibrationAnimDone` prevents restart on chained path segments). Initial vibration y=6: alternating signs, positive-amplitude reductions6→4→2→1, then 8 ±1 cycles = **22 vibration callbacks**. Stop in `MoveSecondHalf` **5464** on reaching the endpoint of the final segment (`nextPathIndex` invalid). It persists over floor loading and chained segments; no endpoint clunk. |
| Event-command moving platform | `SEQ_SE_PL_FW089B_sseq` **1482** | `EventCmdMovePlatform_BeginMovement` **6502**, before vibration; initial y=3, reduces3→2→1 then 4 ±1 cycles = **12 vibration callbacks**. Follow data-driven movement deltas until all offset coordinates reach their targets. `EventCmdMovePlatform_EndMovement` **6650** stops after player coordinates/orientation are committed. No stop at vibration end and no fixed total duration; path-specific distance/speed determine it. |
| Giratina shadow passes | `SEQ_SE_DP_FW019_sseq` **1609**, or Giratina cry | `DistWorldGiratinaShadowProp_AnimTick` **6849/6851**: SFX state chooses normal Giratina cry for CRY, 1609 for FLEE; then falls through to the **first movement update in the same callback**. No explicit effect wait. |
| Boulder falls B5F→B6F | `SEQ_SE_DP_UG_008_sseq` **1571** | `DistWorldFallingBoulder_TickToB6F` **7585**, when −8 units/update crosses the exact B6F landing plane; clamp/change map identity and persist puzzle state after the sound request. Start at impact, not when pushing off the edge. |
| Boulder drops into correct pit | **1571** | `DistWorldFallingBoulder_TickToCorrectPit` **7705**, when pit placement/identity/flags are committed and seven-callback vibration is prepared. Wrong-pit/reset path does **not** call this SE. |
| Cascade down | `SEQ_SE_PL_FW463_sseq` **1488** | `EventCmdCascadeDown_Init` **8224** starts before initial player/mount rotation; `EventCmdCascadeDown_FinishCascading` **8430** stops when final cascade position/orientation is fixed, **before** MOVE_AWAY's two slow west steps. Entire rotation and fall stay covered by loop. |
| Cascade up | **1488** | `EventCmdCascadeUp_Init` **8546**, stop in ending rotation phase **8727** at final state/orientation commit, before its move-away phase. Do not substitute ordinary HM waterfall SE **1613**. |
| Giratina arrival | `SEQ_SE_PL_GIRA_sseq` **1489** | `EventCmdPlayGiratinaArrival_InitSpriteAndSky` **8924**, only once Giratina's billboard exists: activate tinted sprite/dark sky and start SE, then return LOOP so the **first descent update occurs that same callback**. No explicit stop or wait on this SE. |
| Guardian boulder tutorials | Uxie/Azelf/Mesprit cries | `EventCmdShowUxieBoulderTuto_Init` **9002**, `EventCmdShowAzelfBoulderTuto_Init` **9132**, `EventCmdShowMespritBoulderTuto_Init` **9298**: native cry on tutorial initialization; do not add a SE to replace it. |
| Giratina-room stepping-stone reveal/hide | Stop **1484**, then prop transition restarts via helper | `EventCmdShowGiratinaRoomPlatforms_ShowPlatforms` **9423** stops 1484 just before revealing group 1/2/3, permitting the new group's cue to retrigger. Initial delay 36 decrements **in the init callback via LOOP**, so reveals at `t=35,83,131` from command-init t0. Hide init does same with16: hides group 3/2/1 at `t=15,63,111`, each preceded by stop **9459**; after another 48 callbacks, starts an8-callback final wait. These stops are intentional, not global audio cleanup. |

Sources: [landing](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay009/ov9_02249960.c#L2854-L2908), [ghosts](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay009/ov9_02249960.c#L3710-L3894), [elevator](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay009/ov9_02249960.c#L5191-L5492), [event platforms](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay009/ov9_02249960.c#L6475-L6652), [boulders](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay009/ov9_02249960.c#L7571-L7740), [cascades](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay009/ov9_02249960.c#L8173-L8734), [reveal/hide](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay009/ov9_02249960.c#L9399-L9484), [helper](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay009/ov9_02249960.c#L9749-L9754).

**Hook proposal.** Carry the existing game's `AudioHost` into the future world-owned DW support-jump, ghost, elevator, moving-platform, falling-boulder and cascade tasks described in `distortion-world.md`. Call at these transitions; store each prop's logical SFX state independently from opacity/visibility so camera culling and save reconstruction cannot replay sound. Keep `play-if-inactive` semantics in one DW helper backed by the real deterministic `AudioHost.sePlaying`. VM DW opcodes start/wait for the world task; they do not pre-play a cue. DW's prop animation callbacks currently combine logic with rendering in the decomp; our engine should put the opacity/SFX-state advance into 30 Hz simulation and keep the renderer output-only.

## Spear Pillar cinematic application (separate from ordinary world tasks)

Overlay100 contains the large authored Spear Pillar cinematic, not the normal door/gym/world update. Its callbacks are still main-loop updates; retain local counters and animation gates rather than scheduling the entire scene from its launch time.

- `ov100_021D14A8`, `overlay100/ov100_021D13E4.c:125`: `SEQ_SE_PL_W392_sseq` **1479** at local state 3 counter **100/19/18** for Uxie/Mesprit/Azelf respectively; counter begins0 and the equality test is **before** increment, so these are state 3 callback **101/20/19**. Cries occur at counters145/119/100 (callbacks146/120/101), species 480/481/482, normal cry, pan 0, volume 100.
- `ov100_021D1C44.c:467`: `SEQ_SE_PL_W060_sseq` **1477** in state 12 at **incremented** counters15,45,75,95,115,130,145, each immediately followed by setting the pitch for sequence1477 (track mask0xffff) to integer `(counter / 30)*32 + (counter % 32)*10`. These are repeated starts, not one long unmodified loop. State15 **509** plays `SEQ_SE_PL_W082C_sseq` **1478** at its first callback; Dialga cry at callback 40, pan−80/volume 40 (**517**), Palkia at 65, pan+80/volume 40 (**521**). State16 **559** plays Giratina FIELD_EVENT cry after the printer finishes; state 19 **599** repeats it at incremented counter 238. Preserve cry modifications and pan, not a generic SE replacement.
- `ov100_021D2F0C.c:482–510`, state 6 counter is incremented before tests and is **carried from state 5** (which reaches60); the listed values therefore are scene-counter values, not offsets from state 6 entry. `SEQ_SE_DP_CLIMAX01_sseq` **1746** at counter 80 (pan−70) and 135 (+70); `SEQ_SE_DP_CLIMAX03_sseq` **1747** at 165(−70),220(+70); `SEQ_SE_DP_CLIMAX09_sseq` **1749** at 284,338,406; `SEQ_SE_DP_CLIMAX06_sseq` **1748** at 310,375,432; `SEQ_SE_DP_CLIMAX10_sseq` **1750** at 470(−70),520(+70). Animation completion starts `SEQ_SE_DP_CLIMAX12_sseq` **1751** with pan−70 in state 6 (**524**), then +70 in state 7 (**532**); those last two have animation-dependent timing.

Sources: [guardian cinematic](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay100/ov100_021D13E4.c#L80-L135), [Giratina cinematic](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay100/ov100_021D1C44.c#L450-L606), [climax timeline](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay100/ov100_021D2F0C.c#L460-L538).

**Hook proposal.** This application's scene controller should own these events and dispatch through the same Audio contract when the cinematic is implemented. Preserve pan and per-track pitch through an explicit audio capability if the existing contract cannot express them; do not force these timeline effects into generic overworld footstep hooks. Full animation lengths are user-ROM metadata and remain necessary for its animation-gated calls.

The field-owned Spear Pillar controller preceding that application also has native audio in `overlay006/ov6_0223E140.c`: `ov6_0223FAF8` **1611** starts `SEQ_SE_DP_CLIMAX10_sseq` **1750** when a guardian's complete choreography function returns true; Uxie's normal cry **1119** is on first 20-step interpolation completion, Mesprit's **1342** after brightness returns to zero (followed by a60-callback hold), Azelf's **1483** on its second eight-step interpolation completion, all pan 0/volume 100. Brightness-pulse setup `ov6_02240000` **1983** starts `SEQ_SE_DP_CLIMAX06_sseq` **1748** each call; `ov6_0223E71C` invokes it three times: first at state 1, then after delay 16's decrement goes **below zero** (**17 callbacks**, not16) in state 2 and again17 callbacks later in state 3. `ov6_02240C44` **2795** starts `SEQ_SE_DP_CLIMAX15_sseq` **1752** when attaching the climax effect to a named map object; the dormant updater path in `ov6_0223E574` state 8 **706** starts a72-callback hold after the effect, then initializes the next shake (whose setup plays 1749). **Reachability:** the ordinary mode 0 route goes from state 4 directly to11 (`:673–678`), skipping states5–10. Therefore the three1748 pulses,1752 attachment and later1749 shake in those states are dormant native call paths, not additional cues on the normal Platinum scene. Preserve that distinction if implementing this controller; guardian mode 4 cry/1750 calls above are active. Hook reachable scene transitions and actual animation/interpolation completion, not a global story flag. [Field controller](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay006/ov6_0223E140.c#L628-L818), [guardian completion](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay006/ov6_0223E140.c#L1090-L1670), [pulse/effect setup](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay006/ov6_0223E140.c#L1964-L1984), [climax effect attachment](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay006/ov6_0223E140.c#L2785-L2796).

## Script audio, interaction and item acquisition

`PlaySE` (opcode 0x49), `StopSE` (0x4A), `WaitSE` (0x4B), `PlayCry` (0x4C), `WaitCry` (0x4D), `PlayFanfare` (0x4E) and `WaitFanfare` (0x4F) already have VM handlers in `packages/vm/src/audio-ops.ts`. The first, second, fourth and sixth continue in the same interpreter update; the waits yield and poll the audio host. This agrees with the decomp: `PlaySE`/`StopSE` return FALSE, whereas `WaitSE` installs a predicate and returns TRUE. SE operands resolve variables; fanfare operands are literal halfwords. `WaitSE` is not an animation wait or a fixed delay: the sound layer tests the SDAT player associated with the requested sequence. Preserve that player-level behavior when another SE replaces it. [Script commands](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/scrcmd_sound.c#L100-L163)

A field interaction does **not** imply a universal click supplied by `World.interact()`. Five script macros already insert **`SE_CONFIRM_sseq_3` = 1500** before doing anything else: `NPCMessage`, `EventMessage`, `PokemonCryAndMessage`, `PokeMartCommonWithGreeting`, `PokeMartSpecialtiesWithGreeting`. The Pokémon macro waits for confirmation SE completion before its species cry, then waits for that cry before accepting the closing button. The importer decodes expanded bytecode, so adding a second generic interaction click would duplicate these. [Shared interaction macros](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/asm/macros/scrcmd.inc#L5191-L5242)

| Event/system | Exact sequence(s), decimal IDs | Call site and timing / proposed hook |
|---|---|---|
| Visible ground item | Initial **1500**; then the pocket-specific fanfare below | `VisibleItems_TryGiveItem` plays confirmation before locking/facing and waits for it before capacity checks. On success, `Common_CheckBagPocketForItem` starts the fanfare **before** removing the object and adding the item; found-item text can print during it. `VisibleItems_CheckPlateOrItemPocket` waits for fanfare completion before plate/pocket text. Keep this on the existing imported VM path; do not play a new pickup SE from `AddItem`. [Visible pickup](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/res/field/scripts/scripts_visible_items.s#L2297-L2332) |
| Hidden item | Pocket-specific fanfare; an initial **1500** is waited for | The hidden-item script begins with `WaitSE 1500`, not `PlaySE`; its interaction caller owns any preceding sound. Fanfare starts before adding the item and is waited for before plate/pocket text. Do not make `WaitSE` itself emit sound. [Hidden pickup](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/res/field/scripts/scripts_hidden_items.s#L6-L37) |
| Ordinary item, medicine, Ball, battle item, berry received | `SEQ_FANFA4_sseq` **1158** | `CommonScript_GetBagPocketForItem` dispatches by actual item pocket. [Dispatcher](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/res/field/scripts/scripts_common.s#L278-L305) |
| Key item received | `SEQ_FANFA3_sseq` **1157** | Same dispatcher. |
| Mail received | `SEQ_FANFA2_sseq` **1160** | Same dispatcher. |
| TM/HM received | `SEQ_WAZA_sseq` **1163** | Same dispatcher; distinguish receipt from teaching a move. |
| Berry harvest | `SEQ_KINOMI_sseq` **1167** | After yes/capacity checks and picked-berry text, play and wait for fanfare; only **after that wait** does `HarvestBerry` mutate the patch. Ordinary receiving a berry uses 1158 instead. [Harvest](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/res/field/scripts/scripts_berry_tree_interaction.s#L133-L154) |
| Pokétch app received | `SEQ_POCKETCH_sseq` **1162** | `CommonScript_GivePoketchApp`: fanfare starts, obtained-app message is selected, fanfare is waited for, then the app is registered. [Common flow](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/res/field/scripts/scripts_common.s#L548-L559) |
| Accessory received | `SEQ_ACCE_sseq` **1164** | Common accessory flow plays before received text, waits before subsequent storage text. [Accessory](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/res/field/scripts/scripts_common.s#L748-L758) |
| Successful field save | `SEQ_SE_DP_SAVE_sseq` **1563** | `CommonScript_SaveComplete` displays success text, plays SE and waits for it; **then** `WaitABPressTime 30` permits A/B to dismiss or expires after 30 logic ticks (1 s). The earlier 2-tick (66.7 ms) save preamble belongs before saving, not between success text and sound. Hook the save menu's success transition too, because our menu service can save without executing this script. [Save flow](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/res/field/scripts/scripts_common.s#L404-L426), [A/B timeout predicate](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/scrcmd.c#L1400-L1416) |
| Rest / home blackout recovery | `SEQ_ASA_sseq` **1166**, fanfare | Home recovery fades out, closes text, plays and waits, heals, then fades in. This is distinct from the healing-machine native task below. [Home recovery](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/res/field/scripts/scripts_common.s#L1165-L1184) |

The script inventory appendix lists every direct field-script SE/fanfare instruction and all five interaction macro invocations. It includes map-specific emote introductions, key items, puzzles, bells, TVs, teleports and story sounds without requiring a second hardcoded event map. Import and execute their existing sound instructions in order; add native hooks only for sound calls made outside scripts.

## Field UI, menus, shop and PC

All following sounds fire on the accepted input or state transition, once. A held key only retriggers when the corresponding input-repeat/cursor callback actually accepts a move. Rendering a highlighted row, opening React components or receiving an ignored direction must not replay a sound.

| Interaction | Sequence → decimal ID | Verified owner/call site and hook |
|---|---|---|
| Open start menu using field input or script | `SEQ_SE_DP_WIN_OPEN_sseq` → **1533** | Field-input branch plays immediately before `StartMenu_Open`; the script-specific entry plays in `StartMenu_OpenFromScript`. Keep the existing game-owned1533 hook for normal X input; add only genuinely separate script entry paths, avoiding double play if their app helpers converge. [Field control](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay005/field_control.c#L327-L334), [Script entry](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/start_menu.c#L327-L331) |
| Start-menu cursor changes | `SEQ_SE_DP_SELECT78_sseq` → **1504** | `StartMenu_Select` calls `Menu_ProcessInputWithSound(...,1504)`. That replaces the movement sound only; confirm/cancel still use 1500. [Start selection](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/start_menu.c#L684-L695), [Menu input](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/menu.c#L116-L159) |
| Generic menu / yes-no, A confirm, B cancel, successful directional move | `SE_CONFIRM_sseq_3` → **1500** | `Menu_ProcessInput`, `Menu_ProcessExternalInput`, `TryMovingCursorAndPlaySound`; a blocked/no-op cursor move is silent. `FieldMenuManager_DeleteWithMenu` also plays confirmation on disposal, so source paths may reissue 1500 in the same update rather than waiting twice. Match accepted transitions, not every keydown. [Generic menu](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/menu.c#L67-L105), [Successful movement only](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/menu.c#L206-L223), [Field disposal](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay005/field_menu.c#L254-L259) |
| Touch yes-no press | `SEQ_SE_DP_BUTTON9_sseq` → **1508** | On `TOUCH_BUTTON_PRESSED`, not button release or hover; preserve separate touch feedback where our touch overlay mirrors it. [Touch callback](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/yes_no_touch_menu.c#L221-L232) |
| Advance a text-printer continuation/page | `SE_CONFIRM_sseq_3` → **1500** | `TextPrinter_Continue` plays on new A/B or enabled touch continuation. This is not a sound for each printed character, and `ScrCmd_WaitABPress` itself does not play one. Hook the actual page/continuation owner in our dialogue service. [Text continuation](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/render_text.c#L380-L390), [Script button wait](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/scrcmd.c#L1388-L1398) |
| Bag row cursor | `SEQ_SE_DP_GASA01_sseq` **1740**, `GASA02` **1741**, `GASA03` **1742** | `ItemListMenuCursorCB` rotates 0→1→2→0 on actual cursor callbacks; the initialization callback is silent. Keep the cycle in transient bag-controller state. [Bag cursor](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/applications/bag/main.c#L1085-L1102) |
| Bag pocket change | `SEQ_SE_DP_BAG_030_sseq` → **1738** | Starts when a left/right pocket change is accepted, before pocket animation; no sound when only one pocket is available. Touch change paths use the same sequence. [Pocket change](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/applications/bag/main.c#L1286-L1323) |
| Bag/shop quantity changes and shop row movement | `SEQ_SE_DP_BAG_004_sseq` → **1592** | Quantity branches and shop cursor callback; shop initialization is silent. [Bag quantity](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/applications/bag/main.c#L2365-L2393), [Shop callback](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay007/shop_menu.c#L698-L705) |

Failed/cancelled transactions do not reach these branches. [Buy](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay007/shop_menu.c#L1190-L1197), [Sell](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/applications/bag/main.c#L2798-L2806) |

Softboiled/Milk Drink plays once on a valid target **and again** when donor HP animation finishes and receiver animation begins. [Item](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/applications/party_menu/callbacks.c#L409-L428), [HP transfer](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/applications/party_menu/main.c#L2480-L2557) |

Counting the first sound at callback 1, second is callback **18**, a **17-tick = 566.7 ms** gap at 30 Hz. [Panel width](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/applications/party_menu/context_menu.c#L483), [State machine](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/applications/party_menu/context_menu.c#L532-L573) |
| Summary confirm/exit; move-detail open; change visible page | `SEQ_SE_DP_DECIDE_sseq` **1501**; `SEQ_SE_DP_SYU01_sseq` **1692**; `SEQ_SE_DP_SELECT5_sseq` **1505** | Input handlers choose 1501/1692 by action; `ChangePage` plays 1505 only after finding a different allowed page. [Main input](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/applications/pokemon_summary_screen/main.c#L580-L637), [Page change](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/applications/pokemon_summary_screen/main.c#L1338-L1348) |
| PC boot, selecting storage/player/professor/Hall-of-Fame PC, shutdown | `SEQ_SE_DP_PC_ON_sseq` **1548**, `PC_LOGIN` **1549**, `PC_LOGOFF` **1550** | Imported common scripts own these: boot SE before boot prop animation; login before selected-PC message; logoff after closing message and before shutdown animation. Keep script ownership. [Boot](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/res/field/scripts/scripts_common.s#L906-L918), [Login](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/res/field/scripts/scripts_common.s#L976-L981), [Shutdown](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/res/field/scripts/scripts_common.s#L1132-L1137) |
| Storage pickup / placement / invalid move | `SEQ_SE_DP_BOX02_sseq` **1515** / `BOX01` **1514** / `BOX03` **1523** | Multi-select pickup when release A commits selected monsters; placement only on valid empty space; invalid location or cancel while carrying uses BOX03. Single-mon branches use the same family; generic confirmations/DECIDE/BUTTON9 are separate transitions (catalogued in the native appendix). [Storage multi-select](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/applications/pc_boxes/box_app_manager.c#L1590-L1686) |

Text can also **own** sound through printer callbacks. The bag callback uses control parameter 3 to play `PC_LOGIN` **1549**, 4 to wait for that player's completion; parameters 1/2 wait for any SE / fanfare. Party-menu text uses parameter 3 for `KON` **1510**, 4 for fanfare `FANFA1` **1155**, and 5 to wait for `KON`; 1/2 again wait for any effect/fanfare. Hook these to the text printer's decoded control tokens at their actual consumption point, including waits, rather than playing a teaching/item sound when the dialog opens. Ordinary `FieldMessage_Print` installs no such callback. [Bag text callback](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/applications/bag/windows.c#L566-L589), [Party text callback](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/applications/party_menu/windows.c#L1223-L1241), [Ordinary field text](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/field_message.c#L48-L65)

The current start-menu cursor/action controller, bag, ordinary party, storage, mart and `MenuDialog` use no sound calls; the game composition already plays 1533 when opening the start menu. `MenuContext.host.sound` is already available through `AppHost`; use it rather than adding a second audio engine or importing world internals. Resolve sequence records by `sourceIndex` (including aliases), emit sound from semantic input/state transitions, and keep transient cursor-sound counters in each controller. Hatch, evolution and Pokétch already contain sound paths and should retain their ownership.

## Healing machine, berries, honey, Radar and Vs. Seeker

For the Pokémon Center task, count the first `START` callback as tick 1. Each ball's `ADD_POKEBALL` state plays **`SEQ_SE_DP_BOWA_sseq` = 1534**, then loads that ball. Ball `i` (zero-based) sounds at **tick `2 + 17i`**. Its wait increments a counter on 15 callbacks and advances on a sixteenth; one add callback makes a 17-tick (566.7 ms) interval. For `n` balls, **`SEQ_ASA_sseq` = 1166** starts with the final ball/screen animations at **tick `2 + 17n`**. Completion requires both animation loops finished **and** fanfare completion; the short-circuit order matters because the fanfare's completion predicate advances its 15-poll tail only after both animation checks are true. Cleanup is a subsequent callback. Do not replace this conjunction with a guessed duration. [Pokécenter state machine](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay006/healing_machine_animation/pokecenter.c#L75-L163), [15-count limit](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/include/overlay006/healing_machine_animation.h#L16)

Our `packages/world/src/heal-machine.ts` already has the 17-tick ball schedule and exposes placed-ball/flash state, but substitutes a fixed **45 ticks** for the final animation/fanfare and never starts either SE. Add sound events on the actual ADD/PLAY_FINAL transitions, and make final completion depend on the animation task and `AudioHost.waitFanfare()`. Leave `PlayPokecenterHealingAnimation` as the waiting VM command; don't additionally put 1166 in `HealParty`, since that state mutation occurs in other contexts. The nurse's common script explicitly runs machine animation before restoring the party. [Nurse flow](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/res/field/scripts/scripts_common.s#L144-L152)

Honey-tree interaction plays **1500** at script entry. Slathering consumes Honey, updates the tree, then waits **10 ticks = 333.3 ms** before its result message; neither that script block nor `src/overlay005/honey_tree.c` supplies a separate slather/shake sound. Preserve the script confirmation and avoid inventing a native honey SE. Berry harvest uses **1167** as detailed above; other berry interaction confirmation comes from its own script entry. [Honey interaction/slather](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/res/field/scripts/scripts_common.s#L460-L529)

Radar activation's successful generation state creates the shaking patches; the **next task-state callback** calls `Sound_PlayBGM(SEQ_KUSAGASA_sseq)` **1150**. This is a **field BGM**, not an SE or fanfare, despite sounding effect-like. Its lifetime must use the field music/chain owner. No Sound_PlayEffect is present in `pokeradar.c`; don't assign a new SE to each patch by guess. [Radar activation](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/pokeradar.c#L425-L453)

Vs. Seeker's usable START state starts the avatar task and **`SE_DP_VS_SEEKER_BEEP_sseq` = 1568** (`seqInfo` symbol `SEQ_SE_DP_UG_005`), resets the battery and then waits for avatar/rematch emotes. Afterwards it polls this effect until playback has ended before ending the avatar task; no fixed timer or explicit StopSE is used here. Missing battery/no viable trainers branch before START and do not emit the beep. Hook the native Vs. Seeker state transition, whether entered from the Bag or Y. [Vs. Seeker states](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay005/vs_seeker.c#L411-L473)

Registered-item Y dispatch itself has no universal menu-open/click SE: it calls the registered item handler and returns; the following, separate X-menu branch emits 1533. Item tasks (rod, bike, Radar, Vs. Seeker, etc.) must own their respective sounds after usability checks. [Y versus menu dispatch](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/overlay005/field_control.c#L327-L334)
