### Pokétch: existing sound hooks to preserve

`packages/menus/src/poketch/system.ts` already resolves sequence source indexes and gates app SE/cry calls while busy/changing apps; `common.ts` contains the indexes and the app models call `ctx.se`. Side-button sounds intentionally bypass the app gate. This is an existing pattern for world audio injection, not a missing field-wide listener. Native [`PoketchSystem_PlaySoundEffect`](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/applications/poketch/poketch_system.c#L661-L677) accepts the call only when appChanging is false and no field task runs; preserve both conditions.

| Event family | ID(s) and decomp symbol suffix | Native call site / timing |
|---|---|---|
| Side button half-press / release | **1649 DENSI04 /1647 DENSI01** | `poketch_graphics.c:497/505` and `:527/535`, with the changed button tilemap. Full-pressed graphics has no extra sound. |
| App control accepted / refused | **1635 POKETCH_003 /1646 BEEP** | Calculator, memo pad, alarm editing, kitchen timer, roulette, link-search controls, backlight and color changer use local event/task calls; invalid actions use BEEP where their controller specifies it. No universal sound on every touchscreen contact. |
| Counter, pedometer reset, party/friendship/matchup button, stopwatch button | **1641 POKETCH_010** | Their accepted action/graphics task; exact files and lines are in the native index. Species-icon touches use the species/form cry where the app requests one. |
| Dowsing scan | **1640 POKETCH_009** | `dowsing_machine/main.c:170/201`, when scan begins; distinguish its two scan states, rather than sounding from ring drawing. |
| Berry/daycare refresh | **1656 DENSI12** | `berry_searcher/graphics.c:181` (literal1656), `daycare_checker/graphics.c:296`. |
| Coin toss / bounce | **1653 DENSI09 /1654 DENSI10** | `coin_toss/graphics.c:172` at toss start; `:191/:194` at the two bounce branches, driven by motion. |
| Roulette spin result | **1642 POKETCH_011** | `roulette/graphics.c:387`, on the state that completes the spin. Its controls use1635/1646 independently. |
| Kitchen timer ringing hands | **1655 DENSI11** | `kitchen_timer/graphics.c:422`: zero timer is initialized with fallthrough into the first increment; first cue on the **eighth callback including init**, then every **8 callbacks**, until stopBeatingHands. |
| Stopwatch warning / explosion | **1641 /1638 POKETCH_006** | `stopwatch/graphics.c:410/:439` warning loop: initializing to threshold then falling through causes an immediate blink/cue; `++count > 6` or `>3` makes later periods **7/4 ticks**. Explosion cue at entry to `Task_BeginExplosion:465`, once; its later flashing is not repeated explosion audio. |
| Matchup choreography | **1643 POKETCH_012 /1644 POKETCH_013 /1645 POKETCH_014** | `matchup_checker/graphics.c:446` executes PLAY_SOUND operands from its local command table. Each movement consumes16 updates; WAIT16 takes16 decrements **plus the next zero-check**. Relative to initialization/first movement scheduling at t0: low compatibility1643 at 16; medium at 16/32; maximum at 16/32/48 then 1645 at 65; incompatible1644 at 33 after movement16+wait17. Keep this app-local timeline. |

Sources: [Pokétch graphics](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/applications/poketch/poketch_graphics.c#L492-L536), [kitchen hands](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/applications/poketch/kitchen_timer/graphics.c#L393-L426), [stopwatch cadence](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/applications/poketch/stopwatch/graphics.c#L385-L476), [matchup command data and interpreter](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/src/applications/poketch/matchup_checker/graphics.c#L349-L491). The call-site index also covers all other explicit calls in the Pokétch subtree, including unused app2 and the link searcher's locally invoked cues; it is not a claim that wireless features are implemented.

## World and VM integration proposal

This is a proposal only; this research change adds no contract, importer output or runtime behavior.

1. **Inject the existing `AudioHost` into `CreateWorld`.** Match the VM's dependency, with `packages/game` passing its already-created `SoundSystem`. World depends on contracts/core only. Build a `sourceIndex → Id` lookup from `registries.sequences`, as existing VM/Pokétch code does, and use it at simulation event sites. A small world-local helper can expose `play(source)`, `stop(source)` and `playing(source)` to `AvatarHost`, gym `FeatureHost`, `BoatHost` and lift tasks. This helper is not a new synth or event bus. Missing pack records follow the existing logged/counting behavior; do not silently swallow them. `0` is an intentional no-cue only where the native caller supplies it.
2. **Keep native and script ownership distinct.** The VM continues executing imported `play_se`, `stop_se`, `wait_se`, `play_fanfare`, `wait_fanfare` and cry instructions at their original positions. Native task handlers emit only their own nested cues: obstacle effect construction, door clip start, lift motor/arrival, fishing update10, HM-cut-in cry phase, and so on. Do not attach an SE to every `remove_object`, warp, item award, menu opening or story-variable change. Shared actor movement owns jump takeoff/landing for both player and NPCs; drawing a new pose is not an audio event.
3. **Put start/stop/poll beside the owning task transition.** Modules already have the needed state: `avatar.ts`, `npc.ts`, `mapchange.ts`, `platform-lift.ts`, `boat.ts`, `fishing.ts`, `heal-machine.ts` and `gyms/*`. Route world-owned HM cut-ins and VM `play_hm_cut_in` through the same timed presenter. Preserve same-callback fallthrough and decrement-before/after-test behavior where tables specify it. Poll SE/fanfare state once per owning update; rendering, visibility, browser focus, AudioWorklet latency and interpolation must not control task progress. Implement currently absent native controllers (Sunyshore/DW/tram/cinematics) with these event transitions when their behavior is added.
4. **Record door sound classes at import.** The open/close distinction depends on native prop-model identity. Import the chosen class or explicit open/close source IDs with the door metadata and let `World.playDoor` use it for both script and automatic warps. Do not infer it from asset filenames or play 1541 for every door. This future output change requires `IMPORTER_VERSION` and a reviewed contract update. The research file alone needs neither version bump.
5. **Keep native player semantics.** `AudioHost.sePlaying` already polls by SDAT player, and `SoundSystem.stopSe` stops matching sequence handles. Task code must not strengthen the former into “exact clip still running” or weaken the latter into “stop all effects.” Preserve repeated same-tick starts in source order; only use a play-if-inactive gate where the native owner does (for example DW ghosts). Give ambient/task loops explicit lifetime and normal/teardown paths; preserve intentional native stops such as DW group reveals, rather than adding stop-all calls on every world transition. Use `playFanfare` for ME jingles; an ordinary SE must not pause BGM.
6. **Extend audio only for a proven missing operation.** Ordinary world hooks need existing play/stop/poll/cry methods. Spear Pillar's panned effects and sequence track-pitch changes need a contract operation if that application is implemented: the output/synth already supports pan/pitch commands, but `AudioHost` has no general SE pan/pitch method. World and VM must not reach into worklet handles to bypass the contract. Retain sequence/player selection and the native pan conversion in that extension.

Suggested first implementation batch: doors/stairs/escalators; shared jumps and accepted movement sounds; Cut/Smash/Strength and other existing traversal phases; gym/lift/boat sounds; UI input and service sounds. These exercise the existing world state machines and avoid making missing scene rendering a prerequisite. Preserve the already-working script, explosion and Pokétch paths throughout.

### Verification for implementation

Use original fixture sound records and an `AudioOutput` command recorder to assert **ordered commands with tick stamps**, alongside task state. Keep ROM checks gated with `OPENSINNOH_NDS_ROM` and the shared `romSlice()` cache. The important assertions are causal boundaries, not an arbitrary “some sound played” assertion:

- A hinged/sliding/store door emits its own opening ID at clip start; only hinged close emits1543. A blocked door interaction does not first emit1537. Stair fallback and escalator paths do not double-play a door cue; escalator starts/stops 1557 once per task.
- Player/NPC/script jumps emit takeoff and landing once at their shared lifecycle points, including silent custom takeoff. Ordinary ground/ice adds no invented step sound. Materials/bump/gear sound only on accepted native action boundaries.
- Cut sound precedes tree removal by the script's seven-tick wait; Rock Smash uses its ten-tick wait. Strength sounds only when a valid push starts; fishing sounds on cast update10; HM cry follows the cut-in's center phase. Rock Climb debris cadence is tested independently of the mount cue.
- Eterna clock/fountain, Veilstone kick+29/impact+4, Pastoria endpoint+1, Canalave/general lift endpoint/clunk and elevator arrival-SE completion assert the exact phase offsets. Restore/reenter a saved puzzle without replaying a one-shot.
- Boat setup, update198 bridge cue, cinematic entry and the distinct Snowpoint path have one owner. Explosion VM trace stays unchanged. DW ghost helper suppresses retriggers on its busy player, group reveal stops intentionally permit a new cue, and cascade/motor loops stop at their named task state.
- Item/PC/heal/save scripts keep their explicit instructions. Fanfare tests distinguish message time, sequence completion and the 15-check tail. Menu cursor, confirm and error cues fire only when input was consumed; mouse/keyboard/touch do not produce duplicate activation sounds.
- Replay with and without a browser audio output produces identical command ticks, world/VM waits and final state; run at 1×/8× and shifted scene start ticks. Never use an audio device clock or recording of ROM audio as a test fixture.

### Coverage boundaries and reproduction

The script index below includes every direct named SE/fanfare instruction in all 305 field-script files containing a direct SE/fanfare command, including legacy/unused scripts. Macro-expanded confirmation sites are listed separately in the script appendix rather than counted as direct `PlaySE` source lines. The native index enumerates the selected field/controller and menu modules; it includes runtime dispatch operands, with their supplying maps explained in the system sections. A lexical call-site inventory alone is not a proof of runtime reachability; native no-cue cases, branching/gating, resource-dependent completion and existing implementations are therefore called out above.

To repeat the reference audit at the pinned revision, search all `res/field/scripts` for anchored `PlaySE|StopSE|WaitSE|PlayFanfare|WaitFanfare` instructions, and search the native modules listed below for `Sound_PlayEffect`, `Sound_PlayPannedEffect`, `Sound_PlayEffectOnPlayer`, `Sound_StopEffect`, `Sound_IsEffectPlaying`, fanfare operations, sequence pan/pitch and `PoketchSystem_PlaySoundEffect`. Resolve aliases by ordered `fileName` occurrences in `seqInfo` (first unsuffixed, later `_1`, `_2`, …), keeping the original zero-based array index even across empty entries. Follow runtime operands to their constructor, table or caller; inspect the surrounding state machine for each timing claim. No binary sound resource needs to be copied or opened to repeat this names/call-site audit.

## Native call-site index

This index enumerates direct effect/fanfare calls and polls in the selected world and field-menu modules, including duplicate calls and runtime operands. A runtime operand is resolved in the system tables above where its owner supplies one. Cries are covered separately by species/form and are not sequence-ID operands in this index. This is a call-site index, not an assertion that every code path is reachable in ordinary play. Battle animations, multiplayer, Underground interiors, contests and standalone minigames are outside this native index; their field-script entry points remain in the script index.

Scan at the pinned revision: **678 calls/polls in 118 C files**. Each module link opens the enclosing source span; operation lists give every exact invocation line; `StopEffect` rows use the named sequence and native fade argument (all field-loop stops discussed above are immediate, 0).

## Appendix: all explicit field-script SE and fanfare call sites

Inventory of every literal `PlaySE`, `StopSE`, `WaitSE`, `PlayFanfare` and `WaitFanfare` invocation under `res/field/scripts/*.s` at `fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c`. 1,141 command sites in 305 files collapse to 613 file/command/operand groups below. Counts: `PlaySE` 840, `StopSE` 6, `WaitSE` 104, `PlayFanfare` 96, `WaitFanfare` 95.

Sequence IDs are zero-based `seqInfo` positions in [the SDAT manifest](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/res/sound/pl_sound_data.json); filename duplicate suffixes are zero-based occurrence ordinals. Thus `SE_CONFIRM_sseq_3` is **1500** (`SEQ_SE_DP_SELECT`), and `SE_DP_VS_SEEKER_BEEP_sseq` is **1568** (`SEQ_SE_DP_UG_005`). Empty sequence cells mean `WaitFanfare`, which polls the shared fanfare state rather than taking a sequence operand. Every listed number is an exact source line; the script link spans the first–last listed occurrence. This is reference metadata, not reproduced game code or dialogue.

The table covers direct script instructions. Shared macros such as `NPCMessage` and `EventMessage` expand additional confirmation SEs; their definitions and VM implications are described above. It does not interpret resource animation frames as executable script commands.

### Macro-expanded confirmation sites

The [five shared interaction macros](https://github.com/pret/pokeplatinum/blob/fe1ea418a7364c93daf0d07ae55e195452d8803c/asm/macros/scrcmd.inc#L5191-L5242) insert **1500** at entry; `PokemonCryAndMessage` also waits for **1500**, starts the supplied species cry, and waits for the cry. Counts: `NPCMessage` 656, `EventMessage` 119, `PokemonCryAndMessage` 50, `PokeMartCommonWithGreeting` 18, `PokeMartSpecialtiesWithGreeting` 21. These 864 invocations add 864 `PlaySE` sites and 50 `WaitSE` sites to the literal table above. The all-source totals including these expansions are therefore **1,704 PlaySE; 154 WaitSE; 6 StopSE; 96 PlayFanfare; 95 WaitFanfare**. Those totals do not multiply a common script by its runtime callers.
